## v0.5.0 runtime smoke blockers — response shape, correction routing, chain timing (2026-08-11)

Branch `fix/v050-runtime-smoke-blockers` on `a569e458`. Shared-runtime rows, driven on this box
(macOS 26.5.1, M4) with the repo `.venv` (CPython 3.11.15).

| Capability | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| A sentence bound never trims a numbered answer to its marker | VERIFIED | Reproduced the pre-fix defect in one call — a 3-part draft under `exact_sentences=1` returned `text='1.' compliant=True`. After: the first real item survives, `1.` is unreachable. Sabotage (marker counted as a sentence again) turns `test_a_bare_enumeration_marker_is_not_a_compliant_one_sentence_answer` red. |
| A clause-scoped length does not bound a multi-request turn | VERIFIED | `parse_response_constraint("Do three things: explain photosynthesis in one sentence, list two colors, and name a fruit.")` returns `None`; the control (`"Answer in one word: is it ready?"`) still returns `exact_words=1`. |
| An incomplete final answer is not shipped as a finished one | VERIFIED | `_validate_final_chat_output("1.")` and `("")` both used to return unchanged; both now return a notice and record `answer_completeness.degenerate=True`. A partial answer keeps its content and carries its state. Zero-width and BOM-only inputs covered. |
| An exact word count is validated whatever noun it is attached to | VERIFIED | `"give me a five-word title"` parsed to `None` before; now `exact_words=5`, a four-word title is a violation the existing repair path handles, and a sentence *about* a five-word title still parses to `None`. |
| A correction turn stays on the light lane | VERIFIED | `classify("what is that? this is not what i have asked", {"chat_surface": True})` was `research` (queen, paid fallback); it is now `chat_conversation` (`role=auto`, `allow_paid_fallback=False`, not summarization). Lookup turns and technical complaints keep their own lanes. |

## One authoritative execution fact (2026-08-15, branch `fix/execution-truth-model-20260815` on `140d1eb8`)

**Method:** measured against the operator's live runtime DB (`~/.vool_runtime_v050`) at the base
commit, then re-measured after driving fresh turns through a **second, isolated daemon** on `:11477`
(own `VOOL_HOME`, mesh `49177`) — the operator's `:11435` was confirmed untouched throughout, same
pid and same commit before and after. Meter and sabotage harness are committed and re-runnable
(`tools/truth/`).

| Capability | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| One real execution produces one authoritative fact | **VERIFIED** | Measured before: 128 live-data tool executions recorded (116 succeeded) and **0** rows for them in `runtime_tool_receipts`; 2,399 signed honesty receipts of which **2** carried a non-empty `executed_tools`, none on 2026-08-15. The sixteen `tool_executed` call sites emit incompatible shapes (live-data a thin `{tool_name, summary}`, web a full signed `action_record`) and the honesty ledger asked a store the live-data lane never writes to, so it signed an empty tool list over turns that had hit the network. `core/execution_truth.py` records one content-addressed fact per execution at `core/runtime_task_events.emit_runtime_event`, the single function every runtime event already passes through. After, on fresh product turns: `live_data.weather_lookup` and `live_data.market_quote` driven through that exact thin shape both produced authoritative facts; `runtime_tool_receipts` stayed at 0, proving the repair does not depend on the store that was missing them. |
| Every store shares one turn identity | **VERIFIED** | The root cause, and not a counter: no store shared a populated identifier with any other — 809 turn receipts and 807 truth-metric records joined on **zero** ids, and only 255 of the 809 carried any turn id at all. One resolver now stamps a canonical `turn_key` on every event before it is stored or streamed. Measured after: **72 of 86** events carry it (base: **0 of 9,671**), and all events of one turn share exactly one value. |
| Activity, signed receipts and the counters cannot disagree | **VERIFIED** | They derive from the same facts (`executed_tools`, `turn_ran_tool`, `model_calls`, `turn_execution_summary`) rather than each reconstructing reality. `/api/runtime/events` now serves the authoritative per-turn summary and the panel reads it instead of scanning the ledger for `tool_executed`/`tool_selected` — a predicate that rendered "No tool ran — answered directly" over 80 turns with recorded retrieval. Measured after against the live endpoint: 4 turns, each with the correct `ran_tool`/`tool_names`/`model_calls`; turns where Activity and the signed receipts disagree: **0**. |
| A dropped execution record fails the truth gate | **VERIFIED** | `verify_execution_truth` holds the ledger against an **independent witness** (the raw event stream, written by a different path for a different reason) — deliberately not against the derived views, which share one source and would agree while being wrong together. `tools/truth/sabotage_execution_truth.py`: **6 sabotages, every one CAUGHT**, each naming the specific test that bit, each mutation hash-verified as landed and every file restored byte-identical with the suite green afterwards. Two of the six are defects the deterministic tests did NOT model and that live product drives found — a lane minting its own `turn_id` splitting a turn across two identities, and the events endpoint re-deriving identity instead of reading the stamped key (twenty turn keys for a session with three real turns). |
| `attempt_disagrees_with_self_report` has a consumer | **PARTIAL** | It was computed and written into every resolution receipt and nothing read it, so a turn could record a disagreement about whether a model ran and no part of the system was any the wiser. The receipt now carries an `execution` block with the authoritative count beside the turn's self-report and an `agrees_with_self_report` verdict — a third party that is neither disputant. Measured after: **8 of 8** turn receipts carry it (base: 0), one reporting a disagreement. **PARTIAL** because the flag is now surfaced and derivable, not yet acted on by a gate that refuses the turn. |

**Boundaries, stated:** two paths write BELOW the stamping seam, straight to `append_runtime_event`.
The live-data node emitter now captures the canonical key on the turn's own thread, as it already
did for `client_turn_id`. `runtime_attempt_*` stays untagged when the caller supplied no turn id —
the retry dispatcher and restart sweep call it with no turn in scope, and inventing a tag there
misfiles their events; a correct absence, not a gap, and none are executions. No
backfill: facts exist only for turns executed after this change, and the legacy stores are left in
place and still readable. The Activity change was verified through the `/api/runtime/events`
payload, not through a rendered browser panel. Near-full repo suite at `1cef975c`: **18,434 passed, 4 failed** (8h53m); all four reproduce
identically at a clean worktree of the base commit `140d1eb8` and each fails in isolation too, so
they are foreign and not order-dependent. `tests/test_production_route_matrix.py` fails collection
at the base commit as well — also pre-existing.

**Cross-lane: shared runtime (`core/`, `storage/migrations.py`) — KAS should re-verify on Windows.**

### Follow-ups resolve against the obligation, not the prior text (2026-08-15)

Branch `fix/followup-obligation-continuity-20260815`, parent `3cc69da7`. Shared runtime, exercised
on the Apple lane. Full reasoning in `VOOL-DELIVERY/RUNTIME_UPGRADE_LEDGER.md`.

| Capability | Status | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A follow-up naming a new subject re-enters the live-data lane | **VERIFIED** | `What about Tallinn?` after a grounded Kaunas turn: provider asked for `tallinn`, planner built exactly `weather_lookup(Tallinn)`, and Tallinn's fixture-only 12 C reaches the answer. Was one of three G1 strict xfails; promoted by hand with the plan-level and reading-level assertions added. Driven through the real `/api/chat` seam in the hermetic child. |
| A comparison resolves every subject the thread accumulated | **VERIFIED** | `Which one is warmer?` observes Kaunas AND Tallinn on that turn and carries both readings. Also proven for an obligation opened by ONE multipart turn (`Riga and Warsaw`), which is a separate write site. Second promoted G1 xfail. |
| Inheritance breaks on an unrelated turn | **VERIFIED** | `Explain RSA encryption to me.` after two grounded weather turns plans nothing and asks nothing; a following bare `and?` does not resurrect the thread. Held across a 13-turn conversation that switched weather → merkle trees → market. |
| A recall may use prior evidence without ending the thread | **VERIFIED** | `what did it say about Riga` answers without re-fetching; the `and now?` after it re-observes Riga. Control: `book me a flight to Riga next Tuesday please` still ends inheritance. |
| A fresh follow-up never serves a stale reading | **VERIFIED** | What is inherited is a REQUEST; the obligation stores what to ask, never what was answered. Asserted directly — no prior temperature, condition, source label or `Source:` string can appear in an inherited request. |
| The same rule carries to a different domain with no new code | **VERIFIED** | `and ethereum?` after a bitcoin quote rebinds; `which is cheaper` aggregates both. The market branch resolves through its own closed alias table, which also refuses `What about Bergen?` on a market obligation. |
| The conversation's goal is the user's words, never a runtime prompt | **VERIFIED** | Gauntlet `g13_state_hygiene` drives three real turns and reads back the persisted `dialogue_sessions` row: no `Grounding observations`, no `adaptive_research`, no JSON. Control asserts the user's own words still reach the goal, so an empty goal cannot pass it. |
| A lowercase unknown noun rebinds | **NOT CLAIMED — named limitation** | `what about bergen?` does not rebind. No local place authority exists (`tools/web/web_research` states this in its own source), so the weather branch's proof of a proper noun is capitalisation. Pinned by a test so the boundary is a decision, not a gap. Market is unaffected. |
| Behaviour under a live model | **UNVERIFIED** | Every result above is deterministic or through the hermetic seam with the model wire and outbound network faked. No live-model drive was run. |

## First convergence candidate (2026-08-16, branch `integration/convergence-candidate-1-20260816` on `140d1eb8`)

Gate (verification truth) + execution/activity truth + follow-up obligation, integrated and proven
together rather than one at a time. Full reasoning in `VOOL-DELIVERY/RUNTIME_UPGRADE_LEDGER.md`.

**Method:** the authoritative gate (`ops/verify.py`, Python 3.12.13 / pytest 9.1.0 / Ruff 0.15.16)
was run at every landing — gate alone, gate + execution, all three, and again at the final commit —
so a regression is attributable to the landing that caused it. Old shard counts from before the gate
commit are not evidence for this tree. Product proof drove fresh conversations through the real
`/api/chat` on a second isolated daemon (own `VOOL_HOME`, port 11488, mesh 49188); the operator's
daemon on 11435 was untouched.

| Capability | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| The three architectures coexist without duplicated ownership | **VERIFIED** | Execution truth is TURN-keyed (`execution_facts.turn_key`); the follow-up obligation is SESSION-keyed (`live_data_obligation_memory.session_id`) and its `source_turn_id` is written for provenance and joined by nothing. Different keys, lifetimes and owners, so neither can rewrite the other's attribution. The two migrations add different tables at different offsets of one DDL script. Every file either lane owns exclusively is byte-identical to its candidate; the six shared files carry both lanes' hunks. |
| The authoritative gate passes on the converged tree | **VERIFIED** | Receipt `outcome: passed`, `mode: authoritative`, both stages returncode 0, at the final commit. Canonical collection **19,483 items across 1,122 files**, including the gauntlet, benchmark, pa_beta and toolsmith suites a single-process run has to skip. The shard union is checked against that manifest, so nothing collected can be silently skipped. |
| The gate no longer fails itself | **VERIFIED** | It did. `ops/verify.py` hung the per-shard runtime root off the caller's artifact root, CI points that at `.verification-logs/` inside the checkout, and a shard's `VOOL_HOME` mints `data/keys/node_signing_key.json` — so a full run wrote four private keys into the working tree and `tests/test_repo_hygiene_check.py` failed on the gate's own output (4518 passed, 2 failed). The same test passes at a clean worktree of `140d1eb8` under the identical interpreter, so it arrived with the gate. Runtime state now goes to a temp dir and is removed; evidence still goes to the artifact root. Reverting the split fails the new guard by name. |
| The gate's product repairs fix the base's own red | **VERIFIED** | At `140d1eb8`: **4 failed / 115 passed** across `test_machine_list_directory_phrasings.py`, `test_multichat_per_chat_state.py`, `test_prompt_assembly_profiles.py`, plus `test_production_route_matrix.py` failing COLLECTION. With the gate landed: **121 passed / 0 failed**, and 17 passed in the file that would not collect. Exactly the five foreign reds the execution lane had recorded against the base. |
| One real execution, one canonical identity, agreeing everywhere | **VERIFIED** | Live drive: 7 executions, 7 facts, 7 distinct fact ids, no duplicates. Every event carried a canonical turn key — **94 of 94** in one chat, **14 of 14** in the other, none missing and none crossing. Activity's per-turn `execution_truth` from `/api/runtime/events` matched the facts table turn-for-turn: the three tool turns `ran_tool=True` naming `live_data.weather_lookup`, the four model turns `ran_tool=False, model_calls=1`. |
| A follow-up obligation survives multi-turn use, and a topic switch ends it | **VERIFIED** | Fresh wording and fresh cities, not the builders' prompts. `how's the weather in Utrecht right now` → `and Verona?` rebound to Verona; `which of the two is milder?` resolved BOTH, slots `["Utrecht","Verona"]`. A bloom-filter question then ran no tool, the obligation stopped at `source_turn_id=tA3`, a bare `and?` did not resurrect it, and `what about Graz?` afterwards no longer entered the live-data lane. The same aggregate wording in a second chat inherited nothing — it asked what "the two" referred to. |
| The seam that tells the panel what ran is guarded | **VERIFIED** | It was not. The panel is JavaScript and reads `execution_truth` off `/api/runtime/events`; replacing that block with a lie (`ran_tool=False`, import still used so Ruff stays quiet) left the **whole authoritative gate green** — 19,483 items, lint clean, `VERIFICATION PASSED` — while every panel was told nothing had run. That is the exact defect the execution lane exists to remove. Now pinned through the real `dispatch_get` and the real `emit_runtime_event`; three mutation shapes fail it by name, including one that drops session scoping and would let two chats sharing a client turn id share an execution. |
| Behaviour on the free cloud lane | **UNVERIFIED** | The isolated `VOOL_HOME` carries no cloud credentials, so the drive ran local-only (`ollama-local:qwen3:4b`). The conversation invariants above are proven on the local lane. |
| The Activity panel as rendered in a browser | **UNVERIFIED** | Verified through the `/api/runtime/events` payload and the new endpoint test, not a rendered panel. |

**Cross-lane: shared runtime (`core/`, `storage/migrations.py`, `apps/`) — KAS should re-verify on Windows.**

## Evidence sufficiency — an attempt is not an observation (2026-08-16, from `eca76ff9`)

**Method:** the reproduction and every regression are driven through the real public chat seam
(`core.web.api.service.dispatch_post`) with only the model wire and the outbound network faked; the
product rows below are a live daemon at the final commit, `127.0.0.1:11436`, isolated
`VOOL_HOME=~/vool/.vool_evidence_app`, reporting `commit=6f4ba8aee0c1 dirty=False`.

| Capability | Apple | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A failed retrieval never authorizes a live fact, a source, or an observation timestamp | **VERIFIED** | Reproduced at `eca76ff9` through the real seam: `what's the weather in Berlin right now?` fetched and answered; `and Porto?` rebound correctly, planned `weather_lookup(Porto)`, the fetch FAILED (Activity `tool_failed`, receipt `status='failed' source_count=0 failure_class='no observation returned'`) — and the reply was `Porto: Cloudy, high 22 C, low 17 C right now. Source: wttr.in, observed 2026-08-07T12:00:00Z.` Invented values, the previous turn's timestamp, a source this turn never reached. Every layer that RECORDED what happened recorded a failure; the two predicates that READ those records to answer "did this turn observe?" answered from the presence of an entry and never opened it — `turn_ran_observations` → True, `turn_has_current_evidence` → True, and `inspect_unsourced_current_claim` came back `requires_current=True asserts_measured_value=True attributes_source=True has_evidence=True` and declined to convict on that one input. `core/observation_evidence.py` now owns the success question for both, judged from the three structural signals every writer already populates (an explicit `ok`, a non-empty failure text, a zero `source_count`) — no status vocabulary, because the runtime's status strings are open-ended and lane-specific. An entry carrying none of those still counts, so user-supplied material and plain notes are evidence exactly as before. **The same turn at the final SHA, live:** "I didn't run any live lookup on this turn, so I can't verify current temperature figures…" — no value, no source, no timestamp. |
| Naming a source is not evidence of having reached it | **VERIFIED** | Second defect, found by the anti-overfit set rather than by the report. A window containing `Source:`/a URL was withdrawn from consideration entirely, so a turn that observed NOTHING shipped `Bergen: Cloudy, 41.7 C right now. Source: wttr.in, observed …` while the **identical sentence without the source line was convicted** — the runtime was strictly safer when a model invented a reading and named nobody. All three premises of that exemption were measured false at the seam: a successful live-data turn reaches it WITH its receipts (`turn_ran_observations=True web_receipts=1`), so the branch is never entered on the path the exemption claimed to protect; and the delegation to `core.unsourced_current_claim` needs `current_information_required`, which the turns producing this shape do not carry, so no guard owned them at all. Exemption removed; what keeps the seam off grounded answers is `turn_ran_observations`, which is the conjunct that actually knows. |
| A lane that fetched says that it fetched | **VERIFIED** | Three lanes composed answers naming their source and timestamp while recording that on no channel any evidence-sufficiency reader consults — harmless only while some exemption happened to cover the answer's shape. Surfaced by the full gate, not by the focused suites: `BTC PRICE?` fetched a real quote and had `Bitcoin: USD 63,791.00 … Source: CoinGecko` replaced with "I didn't run any live lookup on this turn" — a lane calling its own real work a fabrication. The live-info fast path now records its retrieval between the fetch and every exit that renders from it; the retry lane receipts its re-run the same way `apps.vool_agent` receipts the first attempt; the reconstruction lane records an observation rather than a retrieval receipt, because it runs no fetch — it re-renders a SUCCEEDED attempt from typed outcome rows, each reading still carrying the `observed_at` its original fetch returned, which is recall and not stale substitution. Failed lookups are recorded too, as `ok=False`: truthful, and correctly worth nothing. |
| Per-entity binding on a mixed turn | **VERIFIED** | `weather in Berlin and Porto right now?` renders Berlin from its own observation and marks Porto `unavailable` in its own row with the real failure reason; `Warmest current city` is computed only from the entity that resolved. Sabotage both ways — dropping failed rows, and leaking a sibling's result into the failed row — each bites `test_a_mixed_turn_binds_each_reading_to_its_own_entity` and nothing else. |
| The repair does not cost a turn that really observed | **VERIFIED** | Live at the final SHA: `what is the weather in Porto right now?` → a real sourced answer; `what is the current bitcoin price?` → `Bitcoin: USD 62,934.00 (24h change: -0.04%). Source: [CoinGecko](…), retrieved 2026-08-16 10:23 UTC`; `at what temperature does water freeze in celsius?` → `Water freezes at 0 degrees Celsius (0°C).` untouched. In the suites: successful single, successful multi-entity, successful follow-up, static fact, and ordinary non-live turns all keep their answers byte for byte. |
| Nine load-bearing sabotages, zero survivors | **VERIFIED** | Each mutation is proven to have APPLIED (sha256 changed + exact replacement count) before the run and proven restored after, because a string replace that silently matches nothing produces a green run that reads as a surviving guard. Presence-as-observation → 30 red; the distinction removed in `turn_ran_observations` → 8; in `turn_has_current_evidence` → 5; the execution-record `.ok` filter → exactly 1; the attribution exemption restored → 5; the failure-text rule dropped → exactly 1 (the fx lane's only signal); the `source_count` rule → 2; entity binding removed → 1; a sibling's value leaked into the failed row → 1. |
| Authoritative gate at the final commit | **VERIFIED** | `ops/verify.py --workers 4` → `ok lint`, `ok pytest`, `VERIFICATION PASSED` (returncode 0) at `6f4ba8ae`, Python 3.12.13 / pytest 9.1.0 / Ruff 0.15.16. Two earlier gate runs were RED and each red was a real regression this repair caused, fixed at its cause rather than by narrowing a test. |
| Behaviour on the free cloud lane | **UNVERIFIED** | The isolated `VOOL_HOME` carries no cloud credentials; the live drive ran the local/remote-default lane on this box. The invariants are proven deterministically through the real seam and live on this daemon. |
| A live turn whose retrieval genuinely FAILS mid-flight | **PARTIAL** | Forced deterministically at the real seam (the provider returns "no observation") and proven there, including the exact reported conversation. Live, an unresolvable place name does not fail the lane — it falls through to generic web search, which retrieves something real and attributes the source it actually reached, so the failed-fetch path itself could not be provoked live on demand. |

**Cross-lane: shared runtime (`core/`, `apps/`) — KAS should re-verify on Windows.**

## Evidence sufficiency — the successful direction (2026-08-16, from `e9d15df0`)

Found by human test through the shipped test app, and it is the same contract as the row group
above approached from the other side.

| Capability | Apple | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A successful conductor observation reaches canonical evidence exactly once | **VERIFIED** | `Search live web for current BTC price and Gold price, then calculate how many ounces of Gold equal one BTC.` ran `market_quote:bitcoin`, `market_quote:gold` and `quantitative_reasoning`, 3/3 SUCCEEDED, and the visible answer was "I didn't run any live lookup on this turn." Nothing in `core/conductor` writes to a channel `core.observation_evidence` reads, and nothing could: `apps.vool_agent` hands `NodeContext` a shallow COPY of `source_context`. `core/conductor/evidence.py` now derives observations from the typed `NodeOutcome`s and the dispatch site publishes them onto the caller's context. |
| Which nodes count is decided by declaration, not by name | **VERIFIED** | The gate is the `OperationEffect` each operation already declares: `LIVE_OBSERVATION` (weather_lookup, market_quote, fx_quote, place_search, structured_research) and `WORKSPACE_EVIDENCE` (workspace_investigation, conclusion) observe; `COMPUTED_VALUE`, `DERIVED_ANALYSIS`, `KNOWLEDGE_ANSWER`, `GENERATED_CONTENT`, `ACTION_STATUS`, `MISSING_INFORMATION` reason over facts and are never evidence. A drift test walks the whole registry and asserts the classification for every registered operation, and a test registers a brand-new observing family (`telescope_reading`) and proves it is covered without editing the module. No BTC/gold/weather vocabulary appears anywhere in the gate. |
| The failed direction stays closed | **VERIFIED** | `quantitative_reasoning` SUCCEEDING beside two FAILED quotes licenses nothing; a partial result shape is not a usable observation (`NodeOutcome.succeeded` already requires every declared field, and this module inherits rather than loosens it); a failed observation node is recorded `ok=False`, truthful and worth nothing. |
| Evidence lands exactly once | **VERIFIED** | Publication is idempotent by node id against whatever the channel already holds, so a retry or a second call for the same plan cannot inflate the turn's evidence. Sabotaging the dedupe bites `test_evidence_reaches_the_turn_exactly_once` alone. |
| The repair is WIRED, not merely present | **VERIFIED** | It was not, at first. Deleting the publish call from `apps.vool_agent` left the entire new suite green, because every test drove the helper directly — the exact shape of a guard that is proven in isolation and ships dead. A behavioural test through `_maybe_answer_conductor_turn` caught that in one run, and caught a second copy on the way: `bind_provider_deadline` returns "a context copy" and rebinds `source_context` inside the same method, so the publish was writing to a copy of the caller's dict — the same defect one level above the `NodeContext` copy. Both are now load-bearing sabotages. |
| Authoritative gate at the final commit | **VERIFIED** | `ops/verify.py --workers 4` → `ok lint`, `ok pytest`, `VERIFICATION PASSED`, returncode 0. 14 sabotages across both directions of the contract, zero survivors, each hash-verified as landed before its run and restored after. |
| The same request plans differently per lane | **BROKEN — determined, not repaired** | Measured on this build: the local planner (`ollama qwen2.5:7b`) returns no plan at all, so the conductor declines and the typed live-data lane answers the two quotes and never mentions the ratio; the cloud planner (`nemotron 3.5 lightning free`) returns 3 nodes and names the ratio under "Could not be answered". The clause decomposition IS the planner model's output (`clauses = parse_clauses(ask_model(...))`). `_verify_no_invented_content` bounds what a plan may ADD; nothing bounds what it may OMIT, and there is no lane-independent clause set to check against — so **the canonical user obligation set is NOT identical across lanes**. Execution strategy may differ; obligations must not, and today they do. Recorded for its own mission; it does not affect the evidence contract above. |

Caught by the release gauntlet's G4 case, and it needed the static alias loop in `_extract_price_asset_alias` — the last production path consulting no authority at all — routed through the seam. |
| One asset obligation does not erase its siblings | **VERIFIED** | "Tell me the weather in Berlin, write a rhyme about rain, and give me BTC price" plans `market_quote` AND `weather_lookup`, and `rain` does not become an asset. An operation declares `subjects_named` — what its clause NAMES, before any authority or plausibility filter — and the difference against what it SERVED becomes the obligation. |

| Completion is measured against the user, not against the plan | **VERIFIED** | `compose_answer.complete` was `not missing`, where `missing` is "nodes this plan planned that have no outcome" — a plan-against-plan test that cannot observe a shrink by construction. It now also requires every canonical obligation to be discharged by a node that DECLARES its id. Sabotage S2 restores the old expression; the suite goes red. |

Producers now declare `exported_value_fields` and the dependent's briefing carries the fetched figure; the ratio in the fresh Ethereum/Solana case is the runtime's own arithmetic over two fetcher results. |
| Three gates, not one | **VERIFIED** | Repairing the projection alone passes vacuously. `_quantitative_expand` refused a clause whose every figure came from a live sibling (`has_numbers` False → UNRESOLVED at plan time, before any lookup ran), and `_quantitative_run` raised "no numeric facts were established by this message" even with dependency values present. All three were message-keyed; all three now read message facts ∪ dependency exports. A regression test asserts the message states no numbers at all, so it cannot pass on the message's own figures. |
| A dependency that did not succeed contributes nothing | **VERIFIED** | `NodeOutcome.result` is populated BEFORE the required-field check, so a FAILED node carries a partial mapping; the projection filtered on result truthiness rather than on success. Asserted directly against `_derived_facts` with a partial result, and by sabotage S6. |
| Reporting a gap does not cancel the siblings that work | **VERIFIED** | Residue nodes are deliberately excluded from `clause_nodes`, so they never become a dependent's `depends_on`. The first draft did include them and short-circuited the computing clause to DEPENDENCY_FAILED — reporting one unserved subject silently cancelled the work that could still have run, a larger version of the omission being repaired. Four- and six-subject cases assert the served siblings still complete. |
| A surfaced obligation does not push the turn into the discard branch | **VERIFIED** | `apps/vool_agent.py` discards a composition when `answered_count < unserved_count`. Each surfaced obligation raises `unserved_count`, so the floor would have converted "the reader is told what was missed" into "the conductor declines and a narrower lane answers" — a correctness fix becoming a silent behavioural regression. The ratio is now taken over attempted clauses only. Raised by the RED agent and covered by its own control at the production seam. |
| The obligation set is identical across lanes | **VERIFIED (given a plan)** | Built at plan time from the request and the operations' declarations; no session, model, provider or execution context is an input. Asserted over three materially different `NodeContext`s including one with no generation seam. **Boundary:** when the local planner returns no usable plan the conductor declines entirely and the live-data lane answers — that is a model-capability difference in whether the conductor runs at all, and it is unchanged. |
| The mechanism is the registry contract, not market knowledge | **VERIFIED** | A synthetic parts domain — no aliases, no authority check, no network, no vocabulary shared with the reproduction — registers `subjects_named` and `exported_value_fields` and gets both halves of the repair. Obligation counts of three, four and six; independent and dependent siblings; live-plus-stated arithmetic; a turn with no retrieval at all; reordered, sloppy and misspelled phrasings. |
| The floor stays silent where nothing was dropped | **VERIFIED** | Negative controls: an operation that already carries its declined subjects forward is not double-reported; two legitimately different decompositions of one request both report nothing; an operation declaring no `subjects_named` produces no residue; a covered plan reports `reported_obligation_count == 0`. A rule that fires where there is no defect is how a correctness check gets switched off. |
| Eight load-bearing sabotages, zero survivors | **VERIFIED** | Each mutation hash-verified as applied before its run and restored by hash after. Three survived the first matrix and were NOT explained away: the test helper accepted a subject appearing anywhere in the composed text beside a dash, which every one of these turns satisfies for free — the clause a subject was dropped FROM still quotes it. The helper was made mechanical and the conditional completeness assertion made unconditional. A fourth "survivor" was a no-op mutation of my own (`() or f()` short-circuits to `f()`); inverted to `() and f()` it bites. |

**Known boundaries, stated rather than implied.** Clause-level residue applies to MODEL-proposed
plans only — a deterministic plan is built from Turn IR with skips the planner makes deliberately,
and measuring residue over those reported "Do NOT search the web for tech support" as an unanswered
obligation across 28 suites. It reports only sentences Turn IR classifies as a CONTENT request, so
`ACT` directives and `UNKNOWN` statements are excluded and the floor may under-report a dropped
request it cannot recognize — it will never invent one. It measures COVERAGE, not fulfilment: a
clause carrying a request's words but answering it poorly is not detectable here.

**Out of scope, recorded not repaired.** `core.semantic_claim_authority.mention_is_market_authorized`
returns False for `gold` in "…1 ounce of gold in usd" and True in "price of bitcoin and gold" — the
area repaired at `866cf12a`, deliberately untouched; the floor reports the declined subject instead

Bitcoin. `is_ordinary_multi_part_plain_task` sends some live multipart phrasings to the plain-task
lane before the conductor sees them.

running daemon where noted. The live 123-prompt regression at this SHA had **not** finished when

All seven call sites already had the URL in scope. Ledger keeps url/host/status/duration, capped at 256 entries with `remote_fetch_attempts_truncated()` so a short list cannot read as the whole story; a caller passing nothing is still counted, with a visibly empty host. |
| There is exactly one outbound HTTP door | **VERIFIED** | `_open_remote`'s docstring claimed the property was true "by construction… there is nowhere else to open a socket". There was: `tools/web/searxng_client.py` called `urllib.request.urlopen` itself. The door moved to `core.remote_fetch_policy`, beside the veto and ledger, which is what lets a `tools/` client use it without the import cycle that kept it out. `web_research._open_remote` delegates; its eight call sites and `except` clauses are unchanged. |
| A turn that forbids fetching cannot reach SearXNG | **VERIFIED** | Measured with `urlopen` replaced by a recorder, no request issued. After: forbidden → no socket; allowed → searched, and the call appears in `web_calls` with its address. `web.search` is registered straight onto `client.search` and `core/execution/web_tools.py:41` gates only on the global `allow_web_fallback()`, so the client was the only place this could be enforced. |

Also what's the weather in Berlin?"` → 2 clauses; `"What's your name? …"` → **1 clause**, identity ends the turn, weather half dropped. Every apostrophe opened a quote that never closed. Fixed by the rule already used for `.` inside `8,500`/`app.py`. The guard rejected the empty string, not the sentinel. Matching is case-folded; the first version was not, and the parametrised test caught `UNKNOWN` walking through. The Activity event is untouched — only the receipt is refused. |

**Method:** offline. Provider facts verified against live vendor documentation; request construction
checked provider-by-provider; behaviour driven through the real HTTP handlers with an isolated
`VOOL_HOME`. **No call was made to any search provider** — no key was available, and obtaining one
spends money, which is permission-gated. Rows say which of the two they are.

| Capability | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| A pasted key is stored, listed and deleted through the settings endpoint | **VERIFIED** | Driven through the real `dispatch_post`/`dispatch_get` against an isolated `VOOL_HOME`: save → `connected: true`, GET lists the slot, the secret never appears in any GET body, delete → `removed: true`. The closed slot whitelist still refuses arbitrary names (`search.web.../etc/passwd` and `anything.at.all` both 400) and the cloud lane through the same endpoint is unchanged. |
| A real paste is normalized before it is stored | **VERIFIED** | Quotes, a leading `Bearer `, and surrounding whitespace are stripped, so the key that gets sent is the key that was issued. Found and fixed a defect by adversarial test: `"Bearer "` (decoration with no key behind it) normalized to the literal string `Bearer` and was stored as a credential, producing a provider that would fail auth forever while settings showed it connected. It now normalizes to nothing and the endpoint returns 400. |
| A key is matched to its provider without guessing | **VERIFIED** | Four providers have distinguishing prefixes and resolve high-confidence (`tvly-`→tavily, `jina_`→jina, `fc-`→firecrawl, `BSAI`→brave). Serper, Exa and You.com have **no** documented prefix, so a prefix-less key returns `provider: null` with those three as candidates and the UI asks. Storing under a guess would put a good key in a slot that can never authenticate. |
| With no key stored, the provider chain is unchanged | **VERIFIED** | Pinned by test against the pre-feature order re-derived from the same inputs (including the browser-availability drop): `_provider_order()` is byte-identical to what it produced before this change. A user who never pastes a key sees no behaviour difference. |
| A keyed provider leads the chain, and a stale env order cannot bury it | **VERIFIED** | With a key present the provider is first and the existing keyless chain follows in its original relative order as the fallback. `WEB_SEARCH_PROVIDER_ORDER` sets order for the keyless chain only — a launcher-baked env order predates any provider added later (the recorded `google_html` hazard), so the keyed provider is placed after that order is applied. |
| A keyed search cannot bypass the outbound door | **VERIFIED** | The client reaches the network only through `core.remote_fetch_policy.open_remote`, asserted structurally (AST scan of the new module finds no `urlopen`) and behaviourally (under an explicit `allow_remote_fetch: false` the call raises `RemoteFetchRefusedError` and no socket opens). This is the property `tools/web/searxng_client.py` broke on 2026-08-18; the new path does not repeat it. |
| A dead key reads differently from an empty web | **VERIFIED** | 401/403 → `unauthorized`, 429 → `rate_limited`, 402 → `quota_exhausted`, URLError → `unreachable`, non-JSON body → `bad_shape`, each reaching the turn notes as `<provider>_failed:<reason>`. Classification is by HTTP status, so it does not depend on any provider's error-body shape — which matters because four of the seven do not document theirs. |
| The Test button proves the path that answers questions | **VERIFIED (offline)** | The probe runs one real minimal search through the same client the chain uses, not a cheaper health endpoint, so it cannot pass while real searches fail. It is rate-limited per provider (three clicks inside the window issue one call, pinned by test), sends a fixed neutral query rather than anything from the conversation, and never returns key material. |
| A search key survives a runtime-directory wipe | **VERIFIED** | The Keychain has no list-accounts API, so `list_credentials` reads a sidecar index; a wiped runtime dir leaves the key in the Keychain but the row gone. `_RECONCILE_NAMES` is hand-maintained and already omits most LLM slots, so search slots are derived from the provider table instead, pinned by a test that asserts the derived set equals every provider's slot. |
| Any live call to a search provider | **NOT VERIFIED** | No provider was contacted. Every provider fact (endpoint, method, auth header, query/count field, results path, field names) was read from live vendor documentation on 2026-08-19 and the constructed request checked against it, but nothing proves a provider accepts it until a real key is used. Two facts could not be confirmed from primary docs and are handled conservatively rather than guessed: Exa's result-count field name is omitted from the request entirely, and the invalid-key body shape for Brave/Serper/Jina/You.com is never parsed. |
| Reaching the operator's installed app | **VERIFIED** | Shipped into `~/Applications/VOOL.app` on 2026-08-19 by source re-stage (`ops/live_drive/resync.sh`) at `f05c27ec`, then the daemon restarted. Running runtime reports `commit f05c27ececdc`, `build_id 0.5.0-closed-test+macos+f05c27ececdc`. Live against the daemon: `GET /api/search/providers` returns all seven providers; `POST /api/search/detect` classifies a decorated `Bearer "tvly-…"` paste as tavily/high; `POST /api/search/test` returns `no_key` without calling out; the served `/chat` carries the "Web search key" section. **Boundary:** this re-staged SOURCE into the existing self-contained bundle — the embedded CPython and site-packages are unchanged from the 2026-07-24 build (valid because this change adds zero dependencies). A full `--self-contained` rebuild was not run; it needs `uv pip install`, which the key-machine rule forbids without explicit permission. `Info.plist` `NULLABuildId` was corrected separately — `resync.sh` does not touch it, and the launcher compares that baked id against the running commit to decide whether to replace a daemon. |

**Cross-lane:** all of it is shared runtime (`core/`, `tools/`) plus the shared settings page — KAS
should re-verify on Windows, and the Keychain re-index row is Apple-specific by construction.

## 2026-08-22 — kernel grounding invariant + dropped-math (experiment/kernel-laws-poc)

**Method:** live `qwen3:8b` drives through `run_turn` (S7/S9/S10/S14 fresh mixed-regression
scenarios), 4730 offline kernel tests (repo `.venv`), and 3 adversarial multi-agent review rounds.
Shared runtime (`core/kernel/repl.py`) — KAS re-verifies on Windows. Not integrated to `main`.

| Capability | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| A slot the model re-typed with wrong operators no longer drops the kernel's correct value | **VERIFIED** | Live: S9 `(58×13)−194=560`, S10 `(992÷16)+38=100` & hotel `=512`, S14 `127×19−512=1901` & `166×2+39=371` all ship grounded from the extract-time calc; the txn is reconciled so `COMMIT: committed` and the render agree. Was a silent drop before. |
| A cross-slot / mis-bound calc never ships as an authoritative `[receipt:]` fact | **VERIFIED** | One render-boundary demotion pass + a bounded transitive `_calc_self_contained` check: only self-contained calcs (user literals / this slot's own receipts) ground; every cross-slot value ships `[unverified - model memory]`. Final adversarial review round found ZERO grounded-wrong leaks. Live S7 ratio shipped `2.58`/`12.56` flagged, never grounded. |
| A value-baked pseudo-token does not leak raw braces into the answer | **VERIFIED** | The hygiene gate rejects any `{n…}` carrying a numeric value (`{n74402.55}`, `{n.07962}`, `{n$74402.55}`, `{n74402k}`) while passing template fields (`{name}`, `{note}`, `{n,m}`, `{node}`). Pinned by test. |
| A live-value ratio's VALUE is correct | **PARTIAL** | The value can still be the register-blind model's mis-bind (right source, wrong number); it ships FLAGGED, never grounded. ADA/ADA-collapse and cross-slot-operand mis-binds are refused deterministically. Operand-value-consistency (to ground a correct ratio) is DEFERRED. |
| A same-slot single-entity derive on a web_lookup slot cannot ship garbage | **BROKEN** | S14 Edinburgh can sum two readings (`13+55=68`) and ship it; caught by neither the entity-count nor cross-slot guard. Surfaced as an honest OPEN in the last drive but can still ship in others. Needs a derive-lane guard, deliberately not added — per-shape guards proved leaky across two review rounds. |
| Cross-turn recall on the fresh set | **UNVERIFIED** | The fresh-set harness wrote a 3-turn recall scenario as one prompt; recall is unmeasured until the harness is split (S13). |

**Cross-lane:** all shared runtime (`core/kernel/`). The grounding invariant is a single structural
rule (self-contained calcs ground; cross-slot ships flagged) that replaced a fleet of per-shape
derive-guards after adversarial review showed each guard leaked.

gold I can buy" answered with ONLY a gold quote — no weather, no conversion, no unserved note.

## Permission authority is never optional (2026-08-31, branch `build/tool-permission-p0-20260831`, from `bb338f09`)

Full flow + gaps: `docs/TOOL_PERMISSION_AUTHORITY.md`.

| Capability | Before | Now | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Every foreground tool dispatch receives a permission decision | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED (in-process, real seams)** | `execute_tool_intent` called `decide_tool_call` only `if mode_policy_is_active(source_context)`. Measured at `bb338f09` through the real executor and real handlers: a modeless `workspace.write_file` **created the file on disk** and a modeless `sandbox.run_command "touch …"` **created its marker**, with `decide_tool_call` called **zero times** in both. The gate is unconditional now; both calls return `pending_approval` with `executed=False` and neither side effect lands. Spy WRAPS the real policy (never replaces its answer), so a pass is against real policy. |
| Missing / invalid mode resolves to MANUAL, never AUTO | **BROKEN** (resolved to "no decision at all") | **VERIFIED** | `resolve_effective_mode` is the one authority. `None`, `""`, `"super_auto_do_whatever"`, `17`, and the legacy alias `"bypass"` all resolve to `manual`. AUTO was rejected as the default *because* it allows `create_files`/`modify_files`/`run_side_effecting_commands` — it would have left both measured rows executing unasked. |
| HTTP `/api/chat` stamps a controller-owned mode on every turn | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED** | A body with no `mode` left `operating_mode` unset, and unset was the bypass. Every turn now carries `operating_mode` + `operating_mode_revision`. A turn that omits the field keeps the session's existing selection (Auto stays Auto on turn 2); with no record it is `manual`. An explicit `bypass_permissions` without a live grant still returns **409**, not a silent downgrade. |
| A restart cannot reopen the bypass | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED** | `_ACTIVE_MODES` is in-memory: after `reset_mode_permission_state()` (the restart) a same-session modeless write previously ran undecided. It is decided and refused now, file absent. |
| A spoofed / self-minted mode grants nothing | PARTIAL (bypass already failed closed; *absence* did not) | **VERIFIED** | `operating_mode="bypass_permissions"` with a forged token → `manual`, no write. A forged `internal_authority_token` grants nothing. |
| Typed internal scope is the only non-chat exception | did not exist | **VERIFIED** | `grant_internal_authority(label=…, actions={…})`: unguessable (`secrets.token_urlsafe(32)`, Python-callers only), narrow (allows only when **every** classified action is inside the declared set), in-process (dies with the daemon), ceilinged (`ACCESS_SECRETS`, `FINANCIAL_ACTION`, `CHANGE_SECURITY_SETTINGS`, `UNKNOWN_SIDE_EFFECT` stripped at grant AND refused at consult), attributable (label written to the permission event). A `CREATE_FILES` scope does **not** carry command execution — proven by a `touch` that never lands. |
| Auto's existing contract is unchanged | — | **VERIFIED** | New-file write runs; overwrite of an existing file still prompts and the original bytes survive. |
| Read-only work still works | — | **VERIFIED** | Modeless `workspace.read_file` is *decided* and *allowed*, content returned — the control that keeps the fix from being a blanket block. |
| Approval resume completes the turn the new gate raises | — | **VERIFIED** | A modeless write raises a prompt; `resolve_approval(token, "allow")`; the re-run executes and the bytes land. The same token does **not** carry to a different call (`two.txt` still refused). |
| An outward money effect is refused before dispatch | — | **VERIFIED** | Modeless `pay.x402` → `pending_approval`, `dna_pay_and_unlock` never called. Past the gate (approval resolved) the payment tool's own `user_action_required` boundary still holds and still does not pay. |

**Method — in-process, not daemon.** Real `execute_tool_intent` with the real handler stack, the real HTTP `_dispatch_post`, real `resolve_approval`. Assertions are environmental (does the file exist? did the marker appear? did the bytes change?), not prose.

**RED→GREEN:** 12 behavioural failures at `bb338f09` (`validation-logs/tool-permission-p0-20260831/RED_at_base_bb338f09.txt`) → 26/26 green. **Regression:** the 158 affected test modules run at `bb338f09` **and** at this branch — both show the **same 21** pre-existing failures; this branch adds none.

**Sabotage (3, each naming which tests bit):** restore the `if mode_policy_is_active(...)` gate; default the missing mode to AUTO; accept a spoofed bypass without a grant. See `validation-logs/tool-permission-p0-20260831/SABOTAGE.md`.

**UNVERIFIED — stated, not glossed:** no live daemon restart and no browser drive. The operator experience of the new `pending_approval` turns on the chat surface has not been watched working.

**Open on this lane, from `docs/TOOL_PERMISSION_AUTHORITY.md`:** MCP tools classify as `unknown_side_effect` (prompt in Manual, deny in Auto) and need declared `permission_actions`; the channel / CLI / `null://` ingresses still stamp no mode, so `vool web --render-url` now prompts with no UI to answer it; `effect_gateway.decide_network_fetch` is dead code failing open on two `TypeError`s and was deliberately left untouched.

**Cross-lane:** shared runtime (`core/mode_permission_policy.py`, `core/tool_intent_executor.py`, `core/web/api/service.py`) — KAS re-verifies on Windows after merge.

**Method.** Isolated daemon `127.0.0.1:4291`, `VOOL_HOME=~/vool/live-search-ui-proof-home`, `VOOL_DAEMON_BIND_PORT=49181`. The operator's daemon on `4187` (PID 41567) was confirmed listening before and after every restart and was untouched until the handoff. Cherry-picks were **conflict-free**: both source commits sit directly on the base, and the resulting tree is byte-identical to the source branch tip.

**Tests.** 34-case live-search pack: **34 passed**. New `tests/test_live_search_ui_truth.py`: **7 passed, 6 of 7 RED at base** (`ui_truth_RED_at_base_840a2392.txt`; the seventh is a `no_key` control that predates the fix). Integration pack of **101 files** touching every changed module: **18 failed / 2164 passed** here vs **18 failed / 2123 passed** at base — `comm -23` on the failure lists is **empty, zero new**. Browser gate **3/3 rounds**. Focused Ruff and `git diff --check` clean.

**Runtime code changed: none.** Every proof passed against the integrated code exactly as cherry-picked. The additions are tests and the validation record.

**One harness correction, recorded because it nearly became a false defect report.** The first drive reported "Activity shows no provider row" and "no receipt". Both were the instrument, not the product: the work log ships inside a collapsed `<details>` (reading it without *Expand all* returns `Tool action completed · 1 action`), and the receipt fields ride **flat** on the runtime event rather than under `details`. Verified before anything was written up.

**Open defect — found here, root-caused, NOT fixed.** A turn can publish **fabricated current claims behind a green retrieval receipt**. On chat `openclaw:365939aae19b7e5f3a8d` the model answered *before* retrieval ran, so three real dated Google News rows never reached synthesis, and four invented headlines shipped (*"Rust 1.64 Released…"*, a 2022 version) while Activity truthfully said `Keyless search (google_news_rss) · 3 sources` — which is what made the fabrication look sourced. Two root causes, **both verified present unchanged at base `840a2392`**, so this is pre-existing and not introduced by the integration: (1) `requirements_for(...).current_information_required` reads **False** for *"show me recent news coverage about the Rust programming language"* and **True** for *"what is the latest news on the Rust programming language"*, so the unsourced-current-claim guard is off for one phrasing of the same demand; (2) `core.evidence_binding.inspect_evidence_binding` grounds on a **single** shared introduced term — fabricated and real headlines both contain "Released" — and that one-term floor is the module's documented contract with other callers. Pinned by `tests/test_retrieved_evidence_must_reach_the_answer.py`: both causes asserted as facts, the required behaviour marked `xfail(strict=True)` so a fix cannot land while the file still calls it broken. **Tried and reverted:** wiring the already-computed `inspect_evidence_binding` verdict into the current-claim branch (it is computed a few statements later purely to be recorded) — measured at zero new failures across the 101-file pack, and it does **not** fix this case because cause 2 makes the verdict say `grounded=True`. Reverted rather than shipped as a fix that does not fix. The browser gate drives that phrasing every round as a **recorded, non-gating probe**, so the gate's green can never be read as this case passing; it failed 1 of 3 rounds — the guard gap is deterministic, its manifestation depends on the model.

**Also stated.** OpenRouter is unchanged and out of scope per the goal: `/api/cloud/test` returns `state=failed, detail="unreachable"` — the same mislabel class named as open on the row above. Keyed-lane synthesis ran on `ollama-local:qwen2.5:7b` and its kernel-version answers were factually wrong ("5.15.x", "5.4 or later"); that is a model-intelligence failure, not a runtime failure — the same turns retrieved through Brave and receipted correctly.

**Cross-lane:** no shared-runtime change on this lane; tests only. KAS needs no re-verification beyond the row above.

## P0 — mixed demands must EXECUTE, not merely be counted (`build/mixed-demand-p0-20260901`, parent `840a2392`)

Now all three run and compose: the file's second line, `37*19. = 703.`, and the judgement. `tests/test_mixed_demand_execution_p0.py::test_B_*`. |

**Method — in-process, hermetic.** The only stand-in is the model lane's own `ModelExecutionDecision` plus `render_response` (the seam `tests/test_demand_ownership_r1e.py` already uses), so the general-reasoning demand has a deterministic answer. The file, the read, the arithmetic, the decomposition, the registry, the sub-turns and the merge are all real. No socket is opened.

**RED→GREEN:** 9 failed / 1 passed at `840a2392` (`validation-logs/mixed-demand-p0-20260901/RED_at_base_840a2392.txt`) → 10/10 (`GREEN_at_head.txt`). Proof pack `tests/test_mixed_demand_execution_p0.py`: 22/22.

A single unit naming several files keeps the shared line budget.

**Reachability note, stated because it matters:** the front-door finalize law is unreachable through a whole `run_once` for these two lanes — with coverage declared, the demand-owned composite claims first. It is pinned by driving `handle_turn_frontdoor` directly, where sabotaging it reproduces the base incident exactly, and is kept as the backstop for the front-door pass that runs *before* the composite seams.

**Pre-existing defect found, not introduced and not fixed here:** `tests/foundation/test_r5_pipeline_order.py` leaks a request-id ContextVar (`req:http:r5-empty-sr`) that poisons any later `_Harness`-based test in the same process — **15 existing `test_demand_ownership_r1e.py` tests fail at BASE** when it runs first. The regression pack runs it in a separate invocation for that reason.

**Cross-lane:** shared runtime (`core/lane_registry.py`, `core/agent_runtime/*`, `core/turn_contract.py`, `core/web/api/response_control.py`) — KAS re-verifies on Windows after merge. No file owned by the active live-search lane is touched.

### Amendment — the SERVED proof (real HTTP, real local model, real browser DOM)

The section above was proven **in-process**, with the model lane stood in for. Driven
over the product surface the same message took a **different lane** and failed
differently — which is exactly what a stand-in cannot show.

| Capability | Before (served, `3ad2ca6c`) | Now | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| A mixed turn executes every demand **over HTTP** | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED (real daemon, real local model)** | `POST /api/chat` returned `37*19 = 703.` plus *"Could not be answered:"* for the other two, on route `conductor_multi_intent_plan`. Now: the exact second line, `37*19. = 703.`, and a real `qwen2.5:7b` judgement, on `demand_owned_mixed_turn`. |
| A **composite planner** may claim only what it can execute | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED** | `LANE_CATALOG` grants composite planners an unconditional whole-turn claim. The conductor's plan had 3 nodes and could run 1 — `conductor_plan_completed: 1/3 succeeded`, one `agent_node_started`, `execution_truth.ran_tool=false`. It now stands down before dispatch when a demand it can execute **nothing** for is one the registry says another lane owns. |
| The conductor still ships its truthful **partial** | — | **VERIFIED (control)** | The law fires only on a demand with **no** executable node. A real plan of 8 nodes with 6 unresolved keeps its claim, and a demand nothing covers (`call my mum`) still reaches the conductor's honest "cannot". |
| The durable ledger agrees with the served answer | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED** | All three demands served, ledger still read `u1 indeterminate, u2 satisfied, u3 indeterminate` — no receipt, so the sweep guessed from prose, and a file read answers with the **file's** bytes. The lane now files a `slice_answer_record` per executed demand and a dispatch row per demand: `demand_satisfied: 3`, `fulfilled_obligations: 3`. |
| A failed demand is **PARTIAL**, never SUCCEEDED | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED** | With the local lane unreachable the interpretation produced a runtime *notice*; a notice is a non-empty string with no error, so the turn certified `demand_satisfied: 3, unresolved: 0`. Now: file read and arithmetic still served, the failed demand named, `demand_satisfied: 2 / demand_unanswered: 1`, attempt **`PARTIAL_SUCCESS — 1 of 3 demand obligations unanswered`**, dispatch `u3 … FAILED`, and **no** receipt for it. |
| Three fresh chats, no duplicate work, nothing lost | — | **VERIFIED** | 3/3 runs `demand_owned_mixed_turn`, 3/3 satisfied, `{u1:1, u2:1, u3:1}` dispatches and receipts, capability `{u1: workspace_read, u2: arithmetic, u3: unsupported}`, zero failed checks. |
| What the user SEES equals what was committed | — | **VERIFIED** | The real `/chat` page driven through its own composer; `div.msg.assistant span.msg-text` matches the store's committed answer **byte-for-byte after normalization** — sha256 `996445257f599e7916c9fef6750fa92b162f7dc5d66c31dd66e526bdc7d43f16`. |

**Model safety, stated because a standing rule governs it.** The operator's hard rule on this 24 GB machine is **no local model launches** (three OOM freezes, 2026-08-28). None was launched: `qwen2.5:7b` was **already resident**, and `qwen3:0.6b` was **unloaded first** — free memory went from 0.71 GB to **2.03 GB**, so the machine ended with more headroom than it started with. `qwen3:0.6b` was separately verified *wrong* on the judgement demand ("Yes."), which is why the resident 7B was used.

**RED→GREEN (served):** 5 failed at `3ad2ca6c` → 5 passed. **Sabotage:** five seams, separately, each biting and naming its tests, each restored byte-exact — lane dispatch (14 red), whole-turn claiming at the lane seam (1), whole-turn claiming at the output binder (11), **demand dispatch record (3)**, **final composition (1)**. **Regression:** 1 failed / 696 passed / 1 skipped / 1 xfailed; the single failure is identical at base `840a2392`. **Zero new.**

**Model-intelligence limit, not a runtime defect:** the interpretation's *crispness* is the model's. Given the user's own words plus the line, `qwen2.5:7b` answers correctly but sometimes hedges ("jogging is a form of walking at a faster pace"); given an explicit *"Answer Yes or No"* it says *"No, it describes jogging."* VOOL passes the user's words unchanged and must not inject an output instruction the user did not write.

**The next isolated root defect, named not fixed:** the request-id ContextVar leak (`tests/foundation/test_r5_pipeline_order.py` leaves `req:http:r5-empty-sr` bound, breaking every later `_Harness` turn in the process — 15 pre-existing tests fail at base under that ordering). It did not block this proof, so per the amendment it carries a focused `xfail(strict)` detector, `tests/test_request_id_contextvar_leak.py`, which XPASSes the moment somebody fixes it.

### Amendment — capability truth

The served ledger recorded the interpretation demand as **`capability=unsupported` beside `dispatch=SUCCEEDED` and an answer receipt** — three statements that cannot all be true, and committed as evidence at `8365b87a`.

`unsupported` answers a **claim-time** question ("does a deterministic lane claim this unit?"). A discharge ledger is answering a different one ("what ran it?"). The model lane ran it, on route `model_minimal:qwen2.5:7b`; there was no channel carrying that fact, so the pre-dispatch reading was reported as though it were the execution record.

| Capability | Before (`8365b87a`) | Now | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| A served demand names the lane that ran it | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED (served)** | `u3` was `unsupported` with a SUCCEEDED dispatch and a receipt. It is `model_reasoning` / `model_lane` now, on all three fresh served runs. |
| The registry owns the vocabulary | did not exist | **VERIFIED** | `LaneSpec.executes` — the capability a lane *provides when it executes*, declared **only** where it differs from the coverage claim. `model_lane` declares `model_reasoning`; its coverage stays `fallback_plan`, because a fallback that claimed every unit would make every mixed turn read as covered by one lane and nothing would decompose. `executed_capability()` is the resolver. |
| Every route a sub-turn really stamps resolves | **PARTIAL** | **VERIFIED** | `arithmetic` / `pure_arithmetic_expression` resolved to `None` — the lane records `reason="direct_math_fast_path"` and then overwrites `route_reason`, so the *served* fact carried an unmapped name. Mapped now; all three real routes resolve. |
| The executor is a fact, not an inference | did not exist | **VERIFIED** | Each sub-turn's own `route`/`route_reason` rides `TURN_DEMAND_EXECUTORS_KEY`, keyed by task index. The parent never guesses — guessing is how the contradiction was written. |
| The contradiction is **unwritable** | — | **VERIFIED** | `DemandRecord.__post_init__` refuses `capability=unsupported` with a served terminal state. Falsifiable direction pinned: `unsupported` + `failed` stays writable, because that is the honest row when nothing ran it. |
| A failure keeps the capability identified | — | **VERIFIED (served)** | Model lane unreachable: `u3 | unanswered | model_reasoning | FAILED | 0 receipts`, attempt **`PARTIAL_SUCCESS`**. Naming the executor and succeeding are independent facts. |
| An unnameable executor is named as unnameable | — | **VERIFIED** | `CAPABILITY_UNATTRIBUTED` — "something ran it and the route did not resolve" is a different fact from "nothing can serve it", and collapsing the first into the second is exactly what produced this defect. |

**Corrected durable table (served, successful turn):**

| unit | state | capability | dispatch | receipts |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| u1 `Return exactly the second line of notes.txt.` | satisfied | `workspace_read` | SUCCEEDED | 1 |
| u2 `Calculate 37 x 19.` | satisfied | `arithmetic` | SUCCEEDED | 1 |
| u3 `Decide whether that line describes walking.` | satisfied | `model_reasoning` | SUCCEEDED | 1 |

Attempt `SUCCEEDED`. Forced model failure gives the same table with `u3 | unanswered | model_reasoning | FAILED | 0`, attempt `PARTIAL_SUCCESS — 1 of 3 demand obligations unanswered`.

**RED→GREEN:** 1 failed at `8365b87a` → 5 passed. **Sabotage:** seven seams now, each biting and naming its tests, each restored byte-exact — the two new ones are *capability resolution ignored* (6 red) and *the model lane's `executes` declaration removed* (8 red). **Regression:** 1 failed / 711 passed / 1 skipped / 1 xfailed; the single failure is identical at base `840a2392`. **Zero new.**

## `build/grounding-m2-ordering-20260901` — retrieval before current-answer synthesis (2026-09-01)

Base `a2308a26` (tip of `build/live-search-ui-proof-20260901`). Closes the **ordering half**
of the open defect that lane recorded against itself: a turn that needed current information
ran its model call first and its retrieval afterwards, so real dated rows were fetched, paid
for and receipted with `lifecycle=succeeded` behind invented headlines.

| Capability | Before | After | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Governed retrieval runs BEFORE the model call that writes the answer, for a current-information turn | **BROKEN** — measured order `model.call_completed` then `web_retrieval_started` | **VERIFIED** | In-process, real orchestrator: RED at base is the measured defect reproduced, `AssertionError: ['model_call', 'retrieval']`. GREEN at HEAD. Live on the isolated daemon: `web_retrieval_started(0) → web_retrieval_completed(1) → evidence_bound_to_synthesis(2) → model call(3)`, `ordering_holds: True` on **7 of 7** drives that reached this lane. `proofs/grounding-m2-ordering-20260901/live_reasoning_r3.json` |
| Each retrieved evidence set carries a stable id, and each note an id | — (did not exist) | **VERIFIED** | `evset-711332522280420bba4bee0f` with 6 `evnote-*` ids on the live drive. Ids are derived from the turn identity together with the row digests, so the same rows on a different turn mint a different set — asserted directly, and `binding_admitted_for_turn` recomputes the scope rather than trusting the id |
| The answering model call's envelope names exactly which evidence-set / note ids entered its prompt | — (unanswerable) | **VERIFIED** | `evidence_synthesis_binding` on the call's own context, and the claim is checkable against the prompt rather than only against itself: `evidence_set_id_in_model_prompt: True`, `note_ids_in_model_prompt: True` live, plus a minted sentinel term present in the material handed to the call |
| A retrieval receipt proves retrieval only, never grounding | Implicit — nothing separated them | **VERIFIED** | The binding record is a separate schema (`vool.evidence_synthesis_binding.v1`) carrying `proves="prompt_entry"` and `grounded=None`; the retrieval receipt is asserted to contain no `grounded`/`evidence_set_id`/`note_ids`/`proves` key of its own |
| A retrieval that fails or refuses does not reach the model as if it had current evidence | **BROKEN** — the model was called regardless | **VERIFIED** | With the transport dying inside the real governed collector, the receipt reads `status=failed` and **no model call is made at all**; the turn returns typed truth (`mode=current_evidence_retrieval_failed`, `details.evidence_bound=False`). A refusal is reported as a permission fact, distinct from a broken provider |
| A turn that retrieved nothing cannot publish a fabricated current claim | **BROKEN** — the measured fabrication shipped | **VERIFIED** | The answer-before-retrieval fixture returns the exact bytes the measured turn published; with nothing bound they are not publishable and the ungrounded call is never made. Decided from the **binding fact, not the answer's wording**, so a fabrication phrased in a shape no detector recognises is stopped identically |
| DIRECT / no-retrieval latency and the deterministic live-data lanes are unchanged | — | **VERIFIED** | A timeless request keeps `model_call` first and binds nothing; an explicit `allow_remote_fetch: false` boundary does no hoisted retrieval. Live: the fast live-info lane still answers a news question deterministically with no model call at all (`live_r1.json`) |
| Evidence a pre-model lane already retrieved is identified and bound rather than re-fetched | **BROKEN** — found by the live drive | **VERIFIED** | Live drive r2 showed adaptive research putting four real sources into the prompt with no evidence set, no ids and no binding event: the ordering was right and unprovable. Now minted and bound, with `_collect_live_web_notes` asserted **not** called on that path |

**Method.** Isolated daemon `127.0.0.1:4293`, `VOOL_HOME=~/vool/grounding-m2-home`,
`VOOL_DAEMON_BIND_PORT=49183`, and `OLLAMA_HOST` pointed at a dead port so the daemon boots
`Backend: remote_only | Device: cpu` and **no local model can be reached**. OpenRouter verified
live (`/api/cloud/test` → `state=ok, detail=authorized, http_status=200`); synthesis on
`openrouter-byok:nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b:free`, `locality=remote`. The operator's
daemon on `4187` (PID 32916) was confirmed listening before and after every start and was
never touched.

**Tests.** New `tests/test_retrieval_precedes_current_answer_synthesis.py`: **17 passed**;
the behavioural subset run against a clean `a2308a26` worktree is **8 failed / 5 passed**
(`RED_at_base_a2308a26.txt` — the 5 that pass at base are the controls). 244-file regression
pack selected by symbol reachability, run at the final code on both sides: **24 failed /
6868 passed** at HEAD against **24 failed / 6851 passed** at base, failure sets **identical
by name, zero new**; the +17 passes are exactly this lane's tests. Ruff and
`git diff --check` clean.

One file is excluded from both sides and named rather than dropped quietly: `tests/test_v050_fast_path_costs_no_speculative_inference.py`. Its `test_a_genuinely_ambiguous_request_still_reaches_the_model` hangs indefinitely (0% CPU, no output) on this machine while a parallel lane runs concurrent shard processes, and it hangs **identically at base `a2308a26`** — verified by running that single test alone in the base worktree for 10+ minutes. It is also outside this change: its request ("check the reports folder on this machine and tell me the specs") reads `current_information_required: False`, so the prefetch returns before doing anything. Pre-existing and environmental, attributed rather than assumed.

**Sabotage, cut independently.** Disabling the hoist reddens 8 tests including the ordering
headline; removing only the envelope stamp reddens exactly the 3 binding tests **and leaves
the ordering test green**. Ordering and binding are therefore held by different assertions
and neither can mask a regression in the other. `SABOTAGE.md`.

**PARTIAL, stated rather than smoothed over.** The *"answer contains facts from the bound
sources"* axis is **not verified**. On the one live run where the free cloud model returned a
`valid` response it truthfully reported that the retrieved sources did not cover the question
— correct behaviour under the strict grounding frame, but not the demonstration wanted; on the
other runs the free-tier model returned no usable reply and the runtime said so rather than
substituting cached text. That is a model / free-tier limitation, not a runtime one.

**Reachability, measured and stated.** Driving `/api/chat` does **not** reach this lane on
0.5.0. Every current-information turn is claimed upstream — by the deterministic fast
live-info lane, or by the model-tool-intent loop, both of which already retrieve before they
synthesize (recorded in `live_g1/w1/w2/w3/c1.json`, retrieval at positions 4/5 and the model
call at 14–18). The cause is deliberate: `requirements_for(...).forbids_toolless_lane()`
tracks `current_information_required` one-for-one on every phrasing measured, and the lane
policy uses it to route exactly these turns out of the tools-less chat lane. So this lane is
the **safety net for when the tool loop declines the turn** — which is precisely the condition
that produced the measured defect (a local `qwen2.5:7b` that emitted no usable tool intent).
The live drive injects that one condition and leaves everything else real.

**A local model was launched twice before this was caught.** The first two daemon starts
prewarmed `ollama-local:qwen2.5:7b` and the first four `/api/chat` drives synthesized on it,
against the standing cloud-only rule for this machine. Found by reading the model-lane rows
off the drive's own JSON; the daemon was stopped and every later start was pinned to a dead
Ollama endpoint. Prewarm at boot is this build's own behaviour, not something this lane added.

**Cross-lane:** shared-runtime files changed — `core/grounded_synthesis_binding.py` (new),
`core/agent_runtime/current_evidence_prefetch.py` (new),
`core/agent_runtime/turn_reasoning.py` (+77 lines, no deletions). Independent of M1
(`build/grounding-m1-requirements-20260901`, same parent SHA): every request here uses a
phrasing the classifier already reads as current-required at the parent, asserted in the
family so it cannot drift.

## Claim-to-source support — 2026-09-01, branch `build/grounding-m4-claim-support-20260901` (base `a2308a26`)

Closes root cause 2 of the open defect the browser-proof lane recorded ("an answer carries the
evidence the turn retrieved" — BROKEN): `inspect_evidence_binding` grounded an answer on ONE shared
generic word, so four fabricated Rust headlines read `grounded=True` because a real headline also
said "Released". Root cause 1 (the current-information recognizer asymmetry) belongs to the
requirements lane and is untouched here.

| Capability | Before | Now | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| One incidental shared generic word grounds an unrelated answer | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED fixed** | Music-album note vs software claim: `grounded=False`; corpus 6-one-word 0/2 → 2/2 |
| The committed fabrication fixture reads grounded | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED fixed** | `B_r1_open_defect` answer replayed against the real committed rows: `grounded=False`, zero supported claims, `evidence_discarded=True` |
| One supported claim grounds a fabricated sibling | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED fixed** | Mixed answer → `coverage=partial`, the map names exactly the fabricated claim; corpus 7-mixed-claims 0/1 → 1/1 |
| Claim → supporting-source attribution | absent | **VERIFIED** | Two claims / two sources map independently (`note-1`, `note-2`); corpus 8-multi-source 0/1 → 1/1 |
| Vacuous binding spells itself grounded | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED fixed** | Empty answer / echo-notes / no-notes all `grounded=False` (discard verdict still fires only over real evidence); corpus 12-vacuous 0/2 → 2/2 |
| A retrieval receipt counts as support | **BROKEN** | **VERIFIED fixed** | Receipt-shaped note, zero content: `coverage=no_sources`, supports nothing |

**Data model.** `core/claim_support.py` — `ClaimSupportMap{claims[], source_ids[], source_labels{},
coverage}` with `ClaimSupport{claim_id, text, status: supported|unsupported|uncertain,
supporting_sources[], reasons[], anchors}` and typed `ClaimAnchors{entities, numerics, dates,
quotes, terms, currency_markers}`. Composed into `EvidenceBinding` (new `claim_support`,
`coverage`; `grounded == has_evidence AND coverage=="full"`), and `as_dict()` carries the whole map
into the recorded payload / `/api/runtime` trace projection. Public API preserved: same
`inspect_evidence_binding(answer=, notes=, request_text=)` seam (plus optional `as_of`), same
`evidence_discarded` contract for `runtime_task_outcome`. Only `core/evidence_binding.py`,
`core/claim_support.py` (new) and two test files changed — no classifier, ordering,
response_commit, mixed-demand or tool-call file touched.

**Coverage rules.** A claim is supported only by structure appropriate to it: matching
value/version/date, quoted span, or named entity — or two distinctive shared terms for anchor-less
prose. Claims match bound source CONTENT only (`summary`/`live_quote`/`result_title`/…), never
provenance or receipts. Generic publication words ("released", "announced", …) and request-echo
terms never witness support. A value mismatch or an entity the source never mentions refuses that
source. A "today"-class claim is refused by a source whose only dates are >30 days older than
`as_of` (or the newest note date). Unadjudicable prose reads `uncertain` — it never blocks a
covered answer and never counts as support.

**RED→GREEN.** New `tests/test_claim_to_source_support.py`: 23 cases, module-absent + behavioural
RED at base (`validation-logs/grounding-m4-claim-support-20260901/red_at_base.txt`) → 23 passed.
The defect pins in `tests/test_retrieved_evidence_must_reach_the_answer.py` updated to the repaired
verdict; the `xfail(strict=True)` fired on the fix and its marker is removed, exactly as that file
demanded. Full behavioural pack `tests/test_retrieved_evidence_reaches_the_answer.py`: 38/38
unchanged — every fail-open negative control (no notes, no content, question-echo) and every
grounded floor ("23 knots.", "Bergen.", accents) preserved.

**Corpus.** The audit's 56-probe corpus (committed at
`validation-logs/grounding-m4-claim-support-20260901/audit_probes.py`): **24 pass / 32 fail at
base → 30 pass / 26 fail here** — exactly the six rows in this lane's groups (6-one-word,
7-mixed-claims, 8-multi-source, 12-vacuous) flipped; groups 1–5 and 9–11 (requirements, ordering,
receipt-laundering, typed-rescue, stale-resume, final-seam — other lanes) byte-for-byte unchanged.

**Sabotage.** Two, separate, each biting a different named test and reverted: per-claim isolation
collapsed (any supported claim marks coverage full) → the mixed-claims test and the payload test
fail; generic-word filter emptied → the Title-case "Released"-as-entity test fails.
`validation-logs/grounding-m4-claim-support-20260901/sabotage_runs.txt`.

**Suite.** CI shard runner file lists at HEAD vs detached base `a2308a26`, every shard compared:
failure sets line-for-line identical on all four shards — **zero new failures, zero disappeared**
— and every count delta is exactly the tests this lane added (+23 new cases; the strict-xfail pin
+1 passed / −1 xfailed). Two incidents found and closed on the way, both recorded in
`validation-logs/grounding-m4-claim-support-20260901/shard_suite.txt`: the gate's fail-fast kill
had orphaned a `test_f2_f4_f5_sabotage.py` P13 mutation on disk (3 phantom failures until the file
was restored and re-run byte-identical to base), and
`tests/test_machine_find_folder_paths.py::test_a_bare_name_still_reaches_the_disk_search` hangs
identically at HEAD and base on this machine (filesystem walk; sampled blocked in `os.scandir`) —
the one declared `--deselect`, applied to BOTH sides. Repo-wide Ruff is red at base and here alike
(989 pre-existing findings at `a2308a26`); sorted-diff of the two full-repo runs shows this lane
